1102.TT 亞泥 
 煤氣成本走揚收窄利差 短期展望保守

[法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
亞泥成立於 1927 年，核心業務為水泥、熟料的生產與銷售，市場以臺灣及中國大陸
為主公司，另公司也拓展電力與不鏽鋼領域。2026 年營收組成上，大陸水泥 32.5%、
台灣水泥 32.3%、電力 26.1%、不鏽鋼 3.5%、其他 5.5%。   
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
區間操作 37 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，38 
【大盤指數/股價】45857 / 33.45

➢ 2Q26 水泥需求下滑，惟轉投資挹注獲利 
2Q26 營收 178.3 億元，QoQ+20.24%，YoY-4.47%，中國水泥事業出
貨量仍較 2Q25 略微下滑 ， 且報價亦呈現疲軟走勢 ； 台灣水泥事業方面 ，
受到政府信用管制，導致國內新開工案量減少，影響建商開案進度，導
致國內水泥需求下滑；嘉惠電廠 因天然氣價格走揚，利差收窄，影響整
體 獲 利 表 現 。2Q26 毛 利 率 約 14.03% ， 稅 後 淨 利 52.27 億 元 ，
QoQ+176.98%，YoY+78.4%， 主要受惠轉投資獲利成長 ， 再加上2Q26
公司金融資產及負債評價利益增加所帶動，EPS 為 1.57 元。 
➢ 3Q26 煤、氣成本上揚，利差收窄，獲利表現較差 
展望 3Q26，中國大陸水泥部分 ， 預期3Q26 報價將與 2Q26 大致持平 ，
但受煤炭成本上升影響，預估噸毛利將較 2Q26 略微收斂。在台灣水泥
事業方面 ， 科技廠辦對預拌混凝土的穩健需求 ，2H26 預拌表現將持續優
於水泥。嘉惠電力 2H26 恐延續 2Q26 獲利表現趨勢，亦即利差收窄情
況 。 整體而言 ，3Q26 水泥與發電本業雖受制於煤 、 氣燃料成本高而面臨
利潤壓縮，但整體營運仍維持穩健，且透過多元布局及轉投資，分散產
業循環影響。投研部預估 3Q26 營收 169.76 億元，QoQ-4.79%，YoY-
5.1%，毛利率 12.85%，EPS 為 0.72 元，QoQ-51.07%，YoY-32.54%。 
➢ 2026 政策、地緣政治等仍具不確定性，展望相對保守 
預估 2026 年營收 674.45 億元，YoY-5.1%，毛利率 13.47%，稅後淨利
123.88 億元 ，YoY+23.5%，EPS 為 3.62 元 。 預估2027 年營收約 684.21
億元，YoY+1.45%，毛利率約 13.3%，稅後淨利為 108.85 億元，YoY-
12.14%，EPS 為 3.07 元。 
➢ 短期營運承壓，建議區間操作，目標價 37 元 
整體而言，考量中國水泥需求將呈現逐年緩降的趨勢，對於短期中國水
泥事業成長動能保守看待。台灣方面，2026 年政策底部雖已逐漸浮現，
惟房市回溫仍需時間，再加上電廠利差收窄影響獲利表現評價方面，
2026 年成長動能承壓。預估 2026 年淨值為 52.15 元，過往 PB 評價落
在 0.6X~1.25X 區間 ， 目前為0.64X，雖然位於區間下緣 ， 然考量營運多
空紛雜，維持區間操作建議，目標價 37 元(2026F PB 0.7X)。

公司簡介 
亞泥成立於 1927 年，核心業務為水泥、熟料的生產與銷售，市場以臺灣及中國大陸
為主。旗下亞泥中國的產品廣泛應用於基礎建設與房地產項目。公司同時拓展至電力
與不鏽鋼領域，嘉惠電力經營臺灣天然氣發電廠，透過與臺電的長期購電合約確保穩
定運營，遠龍不鏽鋼則專注於高品質不鏽鋼製造，產品應用於建築與工業領域，兩者
有效提升整體盈利能力。2026 年營收組成上，大陸水泥 32.5%、台灣水泥 32.3%、
電力 26.1%、不鏽鋼 3.5%、其他 5.5%。 
圖 1：亞泥營收組合

資料來源：國票投顧整理 
2Q26 水泥需求下滑，惟轉投資挹注獲利 
2Q26 營收 178.3 億元，QoQ+20.24%，YoY-4.47%，由各事業部門觀察，亞泥中國
事業方面，由於中國內需仍相對疲弱，中國水泥事業出貨量仍較 2Q25 略微下滑，且
報價亦呈現疲軟走勢，拖累整體營收表現；台灣水泥事業方面，受到政府信用管制，
導致國內新開工案量減少，加上年初土方之亂，影響建商開案進度，導致國內水泥需
求下滑，拖累該事業群營收表現；嘉惠電廠則進入發電旺季，營收季增逾50%，惟天
然氣價格走揚，嘉惠一期在轉嫁氣價的能力相對較差，導致利差收窄，影響整體獲利
表現。2Q26 毛利率約 14.03%，QoQ+1.02ppts，YoY-4.04ppts，雖然電廠毛利率
下滑，然而受惠於 1Q26 煤炭價格位處低檔，其成本優勢反映在 2Q26 獲利表現上，
故 2Q26 亞泥中國事業噸毛利仍較 1Q26 成長。 2Q26 稅後淨利 52.27 億元，
QoQ+176.98%，YoY+78.4%，主要受惠於轉投資遠東新(1402)及裕民(2606)航運獲
利成長，再加上 2Q26 公司金融資產及負債評價利益增加所帶動，EPS 為 1.57 元。 
3Q26 煤、氣成本上揚，利差收窄，獲利表現較差 
展望 3Q26，水泥事業方面雖然進入產業淡季，依地區來看，在中國大陸水泥部分，
預期 3Q26 報價將與 2Q26 大致持平，主要受惠四川地區拉動，但受煤炭成本上升影
響，預估 3Q26 噸毛利將較 2Q26 略微收斂，此外，黃岡二線若進展順利，預計於
3Q26 投產，可望帶動出貨量。在台灣水泥事業方面，雖 1H26 整體需求衰退 15%，
但隨著信用管制放寬等房市政策底部浮現，加上科技廠辦對預拌混凝土的穩健需求，
2H26 預拌表現將持續優於水泥。而電力事業上，受制於美伊僵局及荷姆茲海峽封鎖
導致天然氣價格上漲，嘉惠電力 2H26 恐延續 2Q26 獲利表現趨勢，亦即利差收窄情

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
況。整體而言，3Q26 水泥與發電本業雖受制於煤、氣燃料成本高而面臨利潤壓縮，
但整體營運仍維持穩健，且透過多元布局及轉投資，分散產業循環影響。投研部預估
3Q26 營收 169.76 億元 ，QoQ-4.79%，YoY-5.1%， 毛利率12.85%，QoQ-1.19ppts，
YoY-3.84ppts， 稅後淨利25.58 億元 ，QoQ-51.07%，YoY-32.54%，EPS 為 0.72 元。 
圖 2：煤炭價格表現相對強勢，不利 3Q26 利差表現

資料來源：WIND, 國票投顧整理 
2026 政策、地緣政治等仍具不確定性，展望相對保守 
展望 2026 年，中國水泥事業方面，雖然整體房市仍較為疲弱，預計仍將導致需求下
滑，然而觀察近期部份一線城市放寬限購限貸，以及三條紅線監管淡化，將有助於限
縮水泥需求跌幅，並且中期而言，中國十五五計畫強化科技自主、國防安全，預期基
礎建設計畫將有結構調整，有助改善產業朝高質化水泥發展。供給面除了前述產能置
換的政策外，短期仍以錯峰生產為最有效限制供給的手段，惟其高度仰賴企業自律，
具一定不確定性。 
整體而言，預期亞泥中國受惠於產能置換，將有助於提高生產效率及能耗，同時由於
產線自湖北轉移自黃岡，其距離較礦山近，也有利於成本上的降低，此外，較高毛利
的骨料也受惠於產能去瓶頸，2026 年產能可望進一步提升並貢獻營收，惟整體出貨
量預估受到產業環境影響，預估將較 2025 年下滑。 
此外，於獲利表現方面，由於近期煤價表現相對強勁，主要受到中東戰爭導致油氣供
給減少，部分燃料需求轉向煤炭，再加上先前山西煤礦意外，中國政府加強安檢，導
致供應收緊，故預期短期煤價不易下滑，全年噸毛利約落在 15 元人民幣。 
台灣水泥事業方面 ， 仍需觀察政府後續政策實施，2026 年因房市信用管制 ， 資金收縮
影響建商購地及開工意願，導致 1H26 水泥需求量下滑，展望後市，考量政策底部已
經慢慢浮現，預期信用管制不易再度收窄，包含信用管制亦已部分條件放寬，加上新
青安政策延續 ， 故預期房市最懷情況已過 ， 隨剛性需求逐漸進場 ， 將有助2H26、2027
年建商開案數量增加，進一步帶動整體水泥需求。另一方面，政府已對越南進口水泥
課徵反傾銷稅，未來亦有望針對印尼、日本提出反傾銷調查，加上廠辦需求，亦有助

### Page 4

- 4 -

個股報告 
於台灣水泥市場供需持穩，故有望看到水泥需求緩步回溫；最後在天然氣方面，由於
台灣有部分天然氣經由荷姆茲海峽運送，故天然氣成本勢必 在地緣政治影響下提高，
使嘉惠電廠利差收窄，不利於獲利表現。 
綜上所述，2026 年仍受到政策、地緣政治等不確定性影響，營收成長動能相對有限，
預估 2026 年營收 674.45 億元，YoY-5.1%，毛利率 13.47%，稅後淨利 123.88 億
元，YoY+23.5%，EPS 為 3.62 元。預估 2027 年營收約 684.21 億元，YoY+1.45%，
毛利率約 13.3%，稅後淨利為 108.85 億元，YoY-12.14%，EPS 為 3.07 元。 
短期營運承壓，建議區間操作，目標價 37 元 
整體而言 ， 雖然中國政府嚴格執行反內捲政策 ， 以及各家廠商進行錯峰生產嚴控產量 ，
惟錯峰生產仍需仰賴企業自律行為，並且考量中國水泥需求將呈現逐年緩降的趨勢，
對於短期中國水泥事業成長動能保守看待。 
台灣方面 ，2026 年政策底部雖已逐漸浮現 ， 為房市回溫仍需時間 ， 短期房市交易仍相
對冷淡，再加上電廠利差收窄影響獲利表現 評價方面，2026 年成長動能承壓。預估
2026 年淨值為 52.15 元，過往 PB 評價落在 0.6X~1.25X 區間，目前為 0.64X，雖然
位於區間下緣，然考量營運多空紛雜，維持區間操作建議，目標價 37 元(2026F PB 
0.7X)。

### Page 2

2Q26 beat: Gigabyte’s 2Q26 revenue was up 41% YoY / 37% QoQ to NT$144bn, or 12% 
/ 9% above our estimate / Bloomberg consensus, which we attribute to the AI server 
business expansion and PC demand pull-in amid memory price hike. GM of 9.6% is lower 
than our estimate and Bloomberg consensus, mainly on the rising shipments of 
rack-level AI server and rising cost of key components (e.g. memory, CPU, PCB, etc). 
2Q26 opex ratio is better than our estimate and Bloomberg consensus, reflecting the 
improving operational efficiency as shipments ramp up. Overall, 2Q26 net income was 
up 117% YoY / 25% QoQ to NT$6.6bn, or 21% higher than our / Bloomberg consensus.

Earnings revision: We factor in Gigabyte’s Jul revenue and 2Q26 result and raise our 
2026-28E net income by 8% / 14% / 13%, mainly on higher revenues. Our 2026-28E 
revenues are revised up by 16% / 22% / 30%, mainly on more AI servers and higher AI 
server racks’ ASP , considering the migration towards next-generation AI server with 
stronger computing power and more complex design. Our GM is lower given the product 
mix toward AI servers. Our OP income raise is smaller than the GP raise given the R&D 
investments to facilitate AI servers technology development. Our NI raise is smaller than 
the OP income raise given the higher interest expense, reflecting the company’s rising 
debt to support the working capital and capex. The company’s operating cash flow is at 
NT$79bn loss in 1H26 (vs. NT$5bn gain in 2025A).

Valuation: We continue to use near-term P/E to derive our 12M TP . Our 2027E target 
P/E is at 14.7x (vs. previously at 16.8x) applied to diluted EPS, still derived from 
correlation of peers’ forward year P/E to avg. EPS YoY (methodology unchanged), 
reflecting the sector de-rating on AI server ODMs amid the model transition and market

concerns on consumer electronics end demand under the high memory costs. With our 
updated target P/E and earnings estimates, our 12M TP is unchanged at NT$487. The 
target P/E of 14.7x is within the company’s historical avg. and avg.+1std forward trading

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Gigabyte 
We are Neutral rated on Gigabyte with a 12-month target price of NT$487. Our TP is 
based on a 14.7x 2027E P/E which is based on PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery, 2) faster-/slower-than-expected gaming PC growth, and 3) 
faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

We expect eMemory to benefit as PUF-PQC adoption appears
set to accelerate, driven by NeoFuse adoption, PQC

eMemory is a leading embedded non-volatile memory (eNVM) and hardware
security IP provider with a royalty-driven business model: The company
generates high-margin recurring royalty revenue through its semiconductor IP

portfolio. We believe eMemory is well positioned to benefit from advanced-node
migration and the emerging post-quantum cryptography (PQC) opportunity (see our
report: Quantum Security – Building Defenses for Q-Day and Beyond), supporting
long-term royalty growth.
Well positioned for advanced-node migration, supporting broader NeoFuse OTP
(one-time programmable memory) adoption: We believe continued migration
toward advanced process technologies is likely to  drive NeoFuse adoption. Higher

wafer values at advanced nodes should further support royalty growth while
creating future NeoPUF (physical unclonable function) opportunities.
Emerging PQC opportunity could create a second long-term royalty engine: As
governments and enterprises transition toward quantum-safe security, hardware-
based key protection is becoming increasingly important. We believe NeoPUF  is well
positioned as a hardware root-of-trust (RoT) solution and could become a

meaningful growth driver alongside OTP.
Initiate at Overweight with a NT$3,330 price target: Our valuation is based on a
residual income model. We forecast a 25% EPS CAGR (2025-28E), driven by royalty
growth, NeoFuse adoption, and security IP.
Key risks to our outlook include: 1) slower-than-expected adoption by key
customers, 2) slower-than-expected PQC adoption, and 3) competition in the PUF-
based PQC solution.

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Investment Summary: An overlooked quantum and IP play
eMemory is a leading provider of embedded non-volatile memory (eNVM) and hardware
security IP, with a business model differentiated by its high-margin, recurring royalty
revenue stream. In our view, the market continues to primarily view eMemory as an OTP
IP provider, with limited recognition of the company's potential exposure to emerging
post-quantum security opportunities. We believe the company is well positioned to

benefit from two structural trends: (1) continued migration toward advanced
semiconductor nodes, which should support broader adoption of NeoFuse OTP solutions,
and (2) the emergence of PQC, which could create a new long-term growth opportunity
for NeoPUF. As governments and enterprises accelerate migration toward quantum-safe
security architectures, we believe hardware-based key protection will become increasingly
important. eMemory's NeoFuse-based PUF technology is well positioned to serve as a

hardware root of trust for PQC-enabled systems. While PUF currently contributes only
~1% of the company's royalties, broader adoption could create a second long-term royalty
engine alongside eMemory's traditional OTP business. In addition, we view NeoFuse as
positioned to benefit from advanced-node migration, while potentially creating pull-
through opportunities for future NeoPUF adoption. In our view, given eMemory's IP-
centric business model, rising wafer values and increasing production volumes at advanced

nodes should also support sustained royalty expansion over time.
NeoPUF may offer advantages in advanced-node scaling, low-
power designs, and next-generation hardware root-of-trust
applications...
Among today's hardware root-of-trust makers, Synopsys is currently the most direct
competitor to eMemory. Following its March 2024 acquisition of Intrinsic ID, Synopsys'
security platform is primarily based on SRAM PUF technology, which derives device-

unique cryptographic keys from the start-up behavior of volatile SRAM cells. From a
system design perspective, SRAM PUF solutions require dedicated SRAM resources and
additional stabilization, helper-data, and error correcting code (ECC) circuitry to
reconstruct stable keys across variations in temperature, voltage, and device aging.
Unlike SRAM PUF, which derives entropy from the power-up behavior of memory cells,
NeoPUF generates its fingerprint from quantum tunneling characteristics created by

randomly distributed oxide defects within the gate oxide. These defects are formed
naturally during semiconductor manufacturing and produce an enormous number of
possible device states, enabling each chip to generate a highly unique and statistically
random silicon fingerprint with randomness approaching the theoretical ideal.
More importantly, NeoPUF's near-zero bit-error rate originates from the permanent nature
of the underlying oxide defects and dangling bonds. Once these atomic-scale defects are

formed during fabrication, they become highly stable physical structures. Reconstructing
the original silicon-oxide bonding network would require temperatures exceeding
approximately 700°C, far beyond any normal operating or qualification condition. As a
result, the tunneling characteristics remain essentially unchanged throughout the lifetime
of the device, allowing the same enrolled root key to be regenerated repeatedly with
virtually identical responses years later.

### Page 6

M
Foundation
6
Exhibit 8: NeoPUF vs. SRAM PUF
Source: PUFsecurity
We believe eMemory's moat extends beyond NeoPUF itself. The company has spent years
developing and validating its security IP portfolio, supported by a broad patent base and
expertise accumulated through multiple technology generations. Given the mission-critical
nature of hardware root-of-trust architectures, customers tend to prioritize proven
reliability over adopting new security technologies, resulting in long qualification cycles

and high switching costs.
OTP Business Benefits from Advanced-Node Migration and
Rising Wafer Values
As semiconductor production continues to migrate toward advanced process nodes, OTP
benefits from increasing eMemory's penetration as conventional eFuse solutions face
growing scaling challenges at smaller geometries. At the same time, royalties account for
the majority of eMemory's earnings and are closely tied to customer production. As
manufacturing shifts toward leading-edge nodes, higher wafer values translate into higher

royalty revenue per design win. In addition, customers upgrading their security
architecture at advanced nodes are increasingly evaluating OTP and PUF solutions
together, creating incremental cross-selling opportunities. Consequently, we believe that
advanced-node migration should support both broader OTP deployment and sustained
royalty growth over the long term.

Investment Thesis
Recent correction creates an attractive entry point: We believe the recent share price
correction was triggered by 1Q26 earnings miss and concerns over near-term OTP royalty
growth. However, we view part of the weakness as timing-related, as 1Q26 royalties had
yet to reflect recent foundry price increases that management expects to benefit results
in 2H26. More importantly, investor attention remains almost entirely focused on the core

OTP business, while assigning little value to NeoPUF and eMemory's positioning within
the emerging post-quantum security ecosystem.
The market is overlooking eMemory's long-standing PUF investment: We believe
investors continue to view eMemory primarily as an OTP IP company, overlooking its
multi-year investment in PUF technology. Unlike many companies that only recently
began positioning around post-quantum security, eMemory has been developing NeoPUF

for years and has already secured commercial deployments that contribute revenue today.
Attractive valuation relative to both security and semiconductor IP peers: We believe
eMemory should be assessed through both the quantum security and semiconductor IP
peer groups given its exposure to hardware security and royalty-driven IP licensing.
Relative to quantum security peers, eMemory stands out as one of the few companies
generating meaningful earnings and cash flow. Relative to semiconductor IP peers, its

valuation does not appear demanding despite offering superior profitability, with above
50% ROE, well above most global IP peers. We therefore believe the company's valuation
premium is supported by its earnings quality and business model.
Why we rate the shares OW: We see a differentiated combination of structural royalty
growth, advanced-node migration exposure, and underappreciated post-quantum security
optionality. While the market primarily values eMemory based on its established OTP

franchise, we believe NeoPUF could emerge as a meaningful second growth engine as
hardware root-of-trust adoption expands in the post-quantum era. As a result, we see the
potential for both earnings growth and a gradual re-rating of the stock over time.
Investment Positives
1.    PQC adoption could create a new long-term growth engine for PUF: As
governments and enterprises accelerate the transition toward PQC, hardware-
based key protection is becoming increasingly important. PUF is emerging as a

critical hardware root-of-trust component, enabling secure generation and
protection of large cryptographic keys required by PQC architectures. While PUF
currently contributes only around 1%  revenue for eMemory, we believe adoption
could accelerate meaningfully over the next few years as security requirements
evolve. More importantly, eMemory's IP business model benefits from strong
customer stickiness. As a result, broader PUF adoption has the potential to

become a structural growth driver rather than a one-off licensing opportunity,
creating a second long-term royalty engine alongside OTP.
2. NeoFuse is positioned to benefit from advanced-node migration, while

### Page 9

M
Foundation
Morgan Stanley Research 9
creating additional pull-through opportunities for NeoPUF: As semiconductor
geometries continue to shrink, conventional eFuse solutions face increasing
scaling challenges related to area efficiency, reliability and process compatibility.
NeoFuse offers an alternative OTP architecture that is designed to be more
suitable for advanced nodes, particularly below 28nm, where customers have
fewer commercially available options. We believe this creates a favorable

competitive position for eMemory, allowing the company to expand OTP
penetration as advanced-node adoption increases. In addition, OTP and PUF are
becoming increasingly complementary rather than standalone solutions. As
customers redesign their security architecture around next-generation OTP
solutions, they are more likely to evaluate PUF at the same decision point,
positioning eMemory to benefit from its integrated OTP and PUF portfolio. This
creates a potential cross-selling opportunity, where broader NeoFuse deployment

not only can expand OTP royalties but could also increase future PUF royalties. In
our view, OTP migration may therefore also serve as an important catalyst for
future PUF adoption.
3. Higher wafer values could drive sustained royalty expansion: Unlike many IP
vendors that primarily rely on upfront licensing revenue, eMemory generates
about 70% of its earnings from royalties tied to customer production. As
semiconductor content continues to migrate toward more advanced process

nodes, wafer costs have increased substantially versus mature-node production.
Because royalty payments are generally linked to wafer utilization and production
volume, rising wafer values can translate into higher royalty revenue per design
win. This dynamic creates a favorable business model characteristic: once IP has
been qualified and designed into a product, eMemory can continue to participate
in customers' production growth with limited incremental operating costs.

Therefore,  continued migration toward advanced nodes and higher wafer ASPs
could support long-term royalty growth and operating leverage.
Investment Concerns
1.     Slower-than-expected adoption by key customers: Leading semiconductor
companies tend to be more conservative when evaluating and deploying new
memory and security architectures due to extensive validation and qualification
requirements. Given the increasingly concentrated nature of semiconductor

shipments, delayed adoption by a handful of major customers could slow
eMemory's revenue penetration at foundries and postpone the ramp-up of royalty
revenue.
2. Slower-than-expected PQC adoption:      If enterprise, cloud, automotive, or
government adoption of PQC progresses more slowly than expected, demand for
PUF-based security solutions may take longer to materialize, delaying NeoPUF's
revenue contribution and associated royalty growth.
3. Geopolitical and trade policy risks: Rising geopolitical risks could become an

additional consideration in customers' technology sourcing decisions. This may
affect the pace of design-win conversion and customer adoption, particularly

### Page 10

M
Foundation
10
among global customers with increasingly diversified supply-chain strategies.

eMemory: Earnings  Outlook
Revenue
We expect eMemory's revenue to grow at a 25.9% CAGR over 2025-28E, driven by rising
OTP penetration in advanced semiconductor applications, continued ASP increases across
its IP portfolio, and an expanding royalty base. We also expect growing adoption of
advanced-node OTP solutions to support both licensing and recurring royalty revenue,
while PUF-related products begin contributing meaningfully from 2027 onwards as

hardware security requirements increase across AI, data center, automotive, and industrial
applications. Looking further ahead, we see PUF and post-quantum security-related
opportunities as emerging growth drivers for eMemory. While commercialization remains
at an early stage, increasing demand for hardware root-of-trust architectures and secure
device authentication should gradually expand the addressable market and support a
higher contribution from security-related IP over time.

EPS
We expect eMemory's EPS to increase from NT$25.99 in 2025 to NT$53.31 in 2028,
representing a 27% CAGR over 2025-28E. Earnings growth should be driven by continued
revenue expansion, a growing mix of higher-margin royalty revenue, and the company's
highly scalable licensing model. Given its asset-light business model and minimal cost of
goods sold, we expect much of the incremental revenue to flow through to the bottom
line, supporting sustained profitability and earnings growth.

How our estimates differ from consensus...
Our 2026-28E EPS estimates are below consensus. As eMemory remains relatively under-
covered by the sell-side, consensus estimates are derived from a relatively small analyst
base. While we remain positive on the company's long-term growth prospects, our
forecasts assume a more gradual earnings trajectory over the forecast period, resulting in
earnings estimates below current consensus expectations.

We derive our price target from a residual income model. We assume an 8.6% cost of equity
(2.0% risk-free rate, 6.0% equity risk premium, and 1.1 beta), a payout ratio of 90%, a
medium-term earnings growth rate of 23.0%, and a terminal growth rate of 4.4%. Based on
these assumptions, our RI model implies an equity value of NT$249bn, or NT$3,330 per

share.

eMemory: Valuation methodology
Our price target is NT$3,330: We derive our price target from a residual income model,
as we believe this methodology is best suited to capturing the long-term value of
eMemory's high-margin IP and royalty business model. We assume an 8.6% cost of equity
(2.0% risk-free rate, 6.0% equity risk premium, and 1.1 beta), a payout ratio of 90%, a
medium-term earnings growth rate of 23.0%, and a terminal growth rate of 4.4%. Based

on these assumptions, our RI model implies an equity value of NT$249bn, or NT$3,330
per share.
Our price target implies 80x 2027e P/E, above the company's historical average of 69x but
below its +1SD valuation band of 97x. We believe this  valuation is warranted by
eMemory's leadership in embedded non-volatile memory and hardware security IP, long-
duration royalty streams, and growing exposure to secular semiconductor security trends,
including post-quantum cryptography and hardware root of trust.

We believe eMemory should be evaluated through both the quantum computing/security
and semiconductor IP peer groups, given its exposure to both hardware security and
semiconductor royalty-driven business models. Relative to quantum and security peers,
eMemory appears attractive on a P/S basis while standing out as one of the few
companies generating meaningful earnings and cash flow. Relative to semiconductor IP
peers, we view Arm as the most relevant benchmark given the similarities in licensing and

royalty business models. Compared with Arm, eMemory's valuation does not appear
particularly demanding despite delivering significantly higher profitability. With a 2027E
return on average equity (ROAE) of 61%, nearly double that of most global IP peers, we
believe eMemory's valuation premium is justified by its superior earnings quality.
Scenario Analysis
Base case: We forecast revenue CAGR of 25.9% over 2025-28E, driven by rising OTP
penetration in advanced semiconductor applications, continued ASP increases across its IP

portfolio, and an expanding royalty base. Under these assumptions, we derive our base-
case valuation of NT$3,330/share.
Bull case: We forecast revenue CAGR of 42% over 2025-28E, supported by successful
NeoPUF adoption among major hyperscale and semiconductor customers. We assume
eMemory expands its penetration into datacenter and AI-related chips, while advanced-
node OTP adoption and royalty growth exceed our base-case expectations. Under this
scenario, we derive our bull-case valuation of NT$4,555/share.

Bear case: We forecast revenue CAGR of 5% over 2025-28E, reflecting slower-than-
expected OTP adoption at advanced nodes. We also assume alternative PUF technologies
gain market traction, limiting NeoPUF penetration and resulting in weaker royalty growth.
Under this scenario, we derive our bear-case valuation of NT$1,865/share.

Company Background
eMemory Technology Inc., founded in 2000 and headquartered in Hsinchu, Taiwan, is a
leading semiconductor IP provider specializing in eNVM and hardware security
technologies. The company develops and licenses a broad portfolio of memory IP
solutions, including OTP, MTP, flash, PUF, and emerging memory technologies, serving
applications across AI, data centers, networking, automotive, industrial, and IoT markets.

With more than 1,350 patents and technology support extending from mature nodes to
advanced 2nm processes, eMemory has established itself as one of the world's leading
memory IP vendors.
The company's core business is built on embedded memory IP solutions, including OTP,
MTP, and flash technologies, which are widely used for code storage, device configuration,
calibration, and security applications across a broad range of semiconductor devices.
Leveraging its long-standing expertise in embedded memory technologies, eMemory has

gradually expanded its product portfolio to include NeoPUF, NeoFuse, NeoEE, NeoMTP,
NeoFlash, and other next-generation memory and security solutions. These products
target growing demand for advanced semiconductor security, AI infrastructure,
automotive electronics, and future PQC-enabled systems.
The company generated 67.5% of FY25 revenue from recurring royalty income and 32.5%
from license fees, reflecting the scalability of its IP licensing business model.

Key Competitors
Globally, eMemory competes with a range of embedded non-volatile memory (eNVM) and
hardware security IP providers. In the OTP and eNVM IP market, Synopsys (through its

Kilopass acquisition) is generally considered one of the company's closest competitors,
while broader semiconductor IP vendors such as Cadence also participate in selected
overlapping markets. In security-related applications, competition comes from companies
including Rambus, Secure-IC and Intrinsic ID, which offer hardware security, root-of-trust
and PUF-based solutions. In Taiwan, companies such as Faraday and M31 have exposure to

### Page 18

M
Foundation
18
selected memory and security IP markets, although their business focus differs from
eMemory's core offerings. The market remains highly fragmented, with competitive
dynamics varying across process technologies, end-applications and customer
requirements, while foundry-provided memory solutions and internally developed IP at
large semiconductor companies also represent important alternative offerings.
Key Suppliers and Customers
eMemory's customers primarily consist of leading global fabless semiconductor

companies, IDMs and memory manufacturers across consumer, communications, data
center and automotive markets. Revenue is generated through a combination of upfront
licensing fees and recurring royalties. Customers first adopt eMemory's IP during the
design stage and subsequently enter mass production through qualified foundries. Royalty
revenue is then collected based on wafer shipments incorporating eMemory's IP, primarily
from foundries such as TSMC and UMC.

Shareholding Structure
As of April 2026, eMemory's shareholder base was primarily composed of long-term
institutional investors. The largest shareholder was Citibank Custody Singapore
Government Investment Corporation (GIC), with a 6.23% stake, followed by Standard
Chartered Custody for Smallcap World Fund, with 5.45%. Other major shareholders
included Fubon Life Insurance (3.89%), the Labor Pension Fund (3.78%), and board
member Chen Li-Chuan (2.97%). The top ten shareholders were predominantly global

institutional investors and domestic financial institutions, reflecting a relatively diversified
ownership structure with no single shareholder holding a controlling stake.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

### Page 1

Eclat (1476.TW)
2Q26 Briefing – Constructive 2H Outlook; Easing Input Costs and 
Healthy New Product Momentum
CITI'S TAKE 
We came away from Eclat’s 2Q26 briefing with a constructive view on the 
2H26 demand outlook, supported by six months of order visibility, healthy 
new product development activity, and new customer wins. Raw material 
headwinds should ease sequentially from 3Q as oil prices normalize and 
price negotiation progress. We slightly trim our earnings estimates 2-3% to 
factor in 2Q26 results. We reiterate Buy with an unchanged TP of NT$460 
(17x NTM P/E). With fundamentals turning more positive h/h, we think at 
11.5x NTM P/E, near the low end of its historical range, downside appears 
limited with a 6% dividend yield providing additional valuation support. 
2H26 outlook remains constructive with 6-month visibility — Mgmt. reiterated 
that order visibility remains 6 months and noted that monthly sales of >NT$3bn is 
achievable. Looking further ahead, mgmt. also maintains a cautiously optimistic 
view on 1H27E, indicating that despite potential uncertainties from geo tension and 
macro conditions, product development requests, customer engagement and long-
term order discussion remain active.
New product momentum stays healthy; wellness contribution at ~15% — New 
product development remains one of the more encouraging messages from the 
briefing. Mgmt. highlighted that: 1) wellness products account for ~15% of sales and 
continue to receive good market feedback, 2) brand customers continue to invest in 
product innovation at a healthy pace, 3) new product development and launch ratios 
continue to trend upward, and 4) Eclat is currently developing products with three 
new brand customers. One has already started placing orders while the other two 
remain under development. 
Margin pressure should ease in 3Q; product mix remains the key swing factor — 
2Q GPM declined q/q mainly due to higher raw material costs and an unfavorable 
mix, as stronger-than-expected carry-over product demand diluted the margin 
benefit from new products. Mgmt. expects raw material pressure to ease in 3Q, 
supported by lower oil prices and ongoing customer price negotiations. The pace of 
margin recovery will still depend on the mix between new and carry-over products. 
Mgmt. will continue to target > 30% GPM in 2H26E. (continued)

favorable, we see limited downside from current levels. In addition, a 6% dividend
yield provides further downside support while investors wait for earnings
momentum to strengthen.

### Page 5

Eclat (1476.TW)
18 August 2026 Citi Research
5
Eclat
Company description
Founded in 1977, Eclat is a niche apparel ODM/OEM with a unique vertical
integration model. Eclat specializes in high-end functional fabrics and
garments for active wear brands. Its largest brand customers include Nike,
Target, lululemon, and UA. In 2024, Eclat's garment capacity was ~80% in
Vietnam and ~20% in Cambodia. It is currently ramping up new facilities in
Indonesia.

Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Eclat. We like Eclat's strong R&D capability in fabrics
and product innovations, which should give Eclat stronger bargaining power
when negotiating tariffs with customers, supporting a steady sales/earnings
trend vs. other suppliers. We expect ongoing strong order strength from
emerging brands, along with new customer acquisition, to drive sales growth
of 9% y/y in 2026E, while product mix upgrade, positive FX, and rising

efficiency should boost solid margin expansion, driving a record EPS in
2026E. We view current risk/reward as attractive and expect a margin
recovery to serve as a positive catalyst.
Valuation
Our target price for Eclat of NT$460 is based on 17x 2H26H-1H27E EPS, 15%
below its historical average in view of persisting uncertainties on
consumption trend and oil price fluctuations.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

research analysts who have prepared this report (i.e., all research analysts listed below other than those identified as employed

### Page 1

M  Research
19 August 2026
Eclat Textile
Diversified customer base supporting growth
Key Points
• New customers and products to support growth into 2H26.
• Margin headwinds should ease vs 2Q26 on lower raw-material cost
pressure.
• Indonesia capacity set to start mass production in 4Q27.
• Favourable 2H comparison base. Eclat maintains broadly stable order
visibility of around six months, although volatility has increased toward
the end of the cycle. New and wellness products have received positive
customer feedback since launch, and their growing contribution should
support gross margins. The company is also working with two to three
new customers, which should lead to shipments over time. We view
positively the company's ability to secure new customers proactively
despite ongoing industry challenges. We expect revenue growth to
accelerate in 2H26, supported by a lower comparison base.
• Raw-material headwinds should ease sequentially. Management
attributed the QoQ gross margin contraction in 2Q to a higher-than-
expected mix of carry-over orders, despite growth in higher-margin
new products, as well as higher raw-material costs. That said, oil prices
have recently stabilised, and the company is negotiating pricing with
brand customers. As a result, we expect gross margin headwinds to ease
relative to 2Q. Overall, we forecast FY26 gross margin of 31.1%, within
management's target range of 28-32%.
• Investing to improve productivity. Eclat expects to direct this year's
and next year's capex towards expanding capacity in Indonesia and
advancing automation across its garment and fabric plants to improve
efficiency. Management expects the new Indonesian capacity to enter
mass production in 4Q27.
Earnings changes: We lift 2026E/27E adjusted net profit by 3.0%/1.3% to
factor in our higher revenue and margin estimates.
Valuation: We cut our target price 12.8% to NT$547.00 (from NT$627.00),
based on an lower multiple of 22x FY26E PER which represents a 6-year
average vs 26 previously (1 standard deviation above average) to factor in
more volatile industry demand
Catalysts: Monthly revenue update, 3Q26 results, management
commentary, outlook from key customers (e.g., Nike, Lululemon, Under
Armour, Gap/Athleta).
Investment Thesis and Recommendation
We expect Eclat's margins to improve further in 2027, driven by product
innovation. Stronger apparel performance should support volume
and drive a potential re-rating. Eclat is well positioned to secure new
customers and orders as consumers preference shifts to smaller brands.
Prior Eclat research.

### Page 2

Mac Eclat Textile
Key Risks to Investment Thesis
• Weaker-than-expected demand recovery, rising labour
costs, order cuts by end customers, and supply-chain
disruptions.
• Geopolitical risks or increasing trade frictions, such as
tariffs.
• Competition among suppliers, leading to lower margins.
Company Description
Eclat is the leading fabric and garment OEM/ODM in Taiwan,
specialising in knitted fabric and garment products in the
production of functional sports/fitness wear. The company

has in-house fabric and garment production. They also sell
part of the produced fabric to third party customers while
keep the remaining in-house for own garment production.
Eclat's client base is well diversified across global leading
brands, including Nike, Under Armour and Lululemon, etc.

Mac Eclat Textile
Earnings estimate revisions
We lift 2026E/27E adjusted net profit by 3.0%/1.3% on our higher sales estimates.
• Revenue: 2026E/27E up 2.0%/1.6% to reflect higher fabric estimate.
• Margins: 2026E/27E gross margins increase by 0.7ppt and 0.4ppt; operating margins
increase 0.4ppt and 0.1ppt respectively, as we expect new product contribution, cost pass
through and other efficiency initiatives to benefit.

Key Quant Findings
The quant model currently holds a reasonably positive
view on Eclat Textile. The strongest style exposure is
Quality, indicating this stock is likely to have a superior and
more stable underlying earnings stream. The weakest style
exposure is Price Momentum, indicating this stock has had
weak medium to long term returns which often persist
into the future.

Recommendation definitions Volatility index definition Financial definitions
Macquarie – Asia and USA
Outperform – expected return >10%
Neutral – expected return from -10% to +10%
Underperform – expected return <-10%
Macquarie – Australia/New Zealand
Outperform – expected return >10%
Neutral – expected return from 0% to 10%
Underperform – expected return <0%
During periods of share price volatility,
recommendations and target prices may
occasionally and temporarily be inconsistent

with the above definitions.
Recommendations – 12 months
12-month target – Expected share price
in 12 months
Valuation – The company's estimated
fair value share price based on the disclosed
valuation methodology
Note: Quant recommendations may differ
from Fundamental Analyst recommendations
This is calculated from the volatility of historical price
movements.
Very high – highest risk – Stock should be expected to
move up or down 60–100% in a year – investors should be

aware this stock is highly speculative.
High – stock should be expected to move up or down at
least 40–60% in a year – investors should be aware this
stock could be speculative.
Medium – stock should be expected to move up or down
at least 25–40% in a year.
Low – stock should be expected to move up or down at
least 15–25% in a year.
* Applicable to select stocks in Asia/Australia/NZ
Note: expected return is reflective of a Medium Volatility
stock and should be assumed to adjust proportionately

with volatility risk
All "Adjusted" data items have had the following
adjustments made:
Added back: goodwill amortisation, provision for
catastrophe reserves, IFRS derivatives & hedging, IFRS
impairments & IFRS interest expense
Excluded: non recurring items, asset revals, property revals,
appraisal value uplift, preference dividends & minority
interests
EPS = adjusted net profit / efpowa*
ROA = adjusted ebit / average total assets
ROA Banks/Insurance = adjusted net profit /average

total assets
ROE = adjusted net profit / average shareholders funds
Gross cashflow = adjusted net profit + depreciation
*equivalent fully paid ordinary weighted average number of
shares

modelled under IFRS (International Financial Reporting

Source:                         Trendforce, Morgan Stanley Research
TV panel prices fell 1% MoM in August: Mainstream 32", 43", 55", 65" and 75" TV
panel prices dropped 3%, 2%, 1%, 0.5% and 0.4%, respectively, for the month of
August. This is consistent with our expectations. While the overall TV demand

remains lukewarm in 2H26 after restocking in 1H26, the sequential price decline has
narrowed a bit this month vs last month, as the seasonal restocking demand for the
year-end promotions started to emerge, and the component supply shortage and
elevated component prices have made panel makers more reluctant on the
benchmark price concessions. Nonetheless, we believe there might be some rebates
offered to bigger customers, which could be a swing factor to panel makers' margins

in 2H26. On a quarterly average basis, we expect TV panel prices to decline by low-
single-digit % QoQ in 3Q26.
IT panel prices were flat in August: Monitor and notebook panel prices stayed flat
MoM during the month. Both applications are  seeing slower demand into  2H26 as
brand customers pulled forward restocking into 1H26 amid concerns on the
component supply. That said, panel makers are under pressure and insisted on
relatively stable pricing. On a quarterly average basis, we expect IT panel prices to

remain flat QoQ in 3Q26.
Risk/reward less attractive: We see current valuation fair  after recent correction,
particularly for Taiwan names AUO (2409.TW; 1.3x P/B) and Innolux (3481.TW; 1.6x
P/B), as incremental contributions from advanced packaging are likely to take longer
to materialize and there might be more competition in the mass production stage, as
various companies in the display supply chain are devoting resources into this area.
In China, we prefer BOE (000725.SZ; 1.6x P/B) over TCL (000100.SZ; 1.8x P/B) on

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Seasonal restocking helped narrowed the TV panel price decline in Aug, though
we think rebates to big customers is a swing factor in panel makers' margin in
2H26
IT panel prices stayed flat MoM in August and likely will in coming quarters.
Buying panel stocks from the angle of glass adoption in advanced packaging does

not seem attractive on risk-reward, in our view.
August 20, 2026 03:05 PM GMT

Morgan Stanley Research 3
TV Panel Prices Trending Down in 3Q26
TV panel prices started to edge up at the beginning of this year, rising 1% MoM in January,
2% MoM in February and 2% MoM in March. April, May and June were largely flat, while
July was down 2% MoM and August was down 1% MoM, in line with our expectation that
TV panel prices would start trending down from 3Q26.  That said, we do not expect a
significant decline as supply-side discipline should provide downside support.

Below-Seasonal Panel Shipments in 2H26
Over the past decade, TV panel shipments have typically been split 49%:51% between 1H
and 2H. In 2026, we expect the mix to skew toward 1H, as TV brands restocked ahead of
major promotional events, including the Winter Olympics in February, the FIFA World Cup
in June-July, and China's 618 shopping festival. We see no clear evidence that these events

will materially boost global TV demand, but they have historically shifted shipment timing
across quarters. As a result, we expect the restocking momentum seen since the start of
the year to fade, with panel order momentum slowing in 2H26.
Display Fab Utilization To Be at ~85% in 3Q26
Stronger TV panel shipments supported display fab utilization of ~80% in 4Q24 and 82%
in 1Q25. Utilization declined to 78% in 2Q25 as brands adopted a more cautious stance

following earlier pull-ins and as the impact of China's trade-in program moderated.
Utilization rebounded to 80-85% in 3Q25 as brands restocked for year-end promotions
and remained at similar levels in 4Q25 despite seasonal softness as well as in 1Q26.
Industry utilization stayed broadly flat QoQ at 80-85% in 2Q26, reflecting continued
supply discipline, while we expect a slight uptick in 3Q26.
Over the medium to long term, we continue to believe the display industry is undergoing

structural change. Chinese panel makers have shifted their focus from top-line growth to
profitability, while lower government subsidies have reinforced supply discipline. We
believe investors increasingly recognize that the industry's production-to-order model can
remain sustainable, particularly as panel price cycles shortened to quarters in 2023-24 vs.
multiyear cycles in the previous decade. Consolidation actions, including LG Display's sale
of its Gen 8.5 Guangzhou fab to TCL and Sharp's closure of its Gen 10 Sakai fab, also

support a healthier supply backdrop (for more information, see Display – Structural

### Page 5

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 5
Risk/Reward Fair After Recent Correction
We view risk-reward as broadly balanced following the recent correction in panel stocks.
Below are our current views:
• AUO (2409.TW, Derrick Yang): We rate AUO Equal-weight with a PT of NT
$27.00, based on a 1.4x 2026e P/B multiple. While we believe the current round of
TV panel price increases is nearing an end, major panel makers should continue to
demonstrate solid supply discipline, providing downside support. We appreciate

AUO's efforts to diversify its business, but meaningful earnings contributions are
likely to take time.
• BOE (000725.SZ, Derrick Yang): We prefer BOE due to its scale advantage,
higher exposure to display, and faster progress in glass core substrates. Our PT of
Rmb9.30 is based on 2.5x 2026e P/B, which we view as reasonable given our
estimated ROE of 5-8% over 2026-28 compared with BOE's average P/B of 1.1x and
ROE of 2-4% during 2023-25.
• Innolux (3481.TW, Derrick Yang): In semiconductors, we believe Innolux is

participating in a foundry customer's glass core substrate project. However, we do
not expect meaningful contributions before 2028, and additional competitors
could enter as the industry moves toward mass production. We maintain our
Equal-weight rating and PT of NT$60.00, based on a 2.1x 2026e P/B multiple.
• LG Display (034220.KS, Ryan Kim): Meaningful OLED revenue contribution
should increasingly improve earnings from 2H25, but potentially weaker end-

markets keep us from turning bullish on LGD. The stock trades at 0.7x 2026e P/B
vs. a historical mid-cycle level of 0.5x.
• TCL (000100.SZ, Derrick Yang): We are EW with a price target of Rmb4.70 (1.7x
2026e P/B). The stock is trading at 1.8x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.4x
since 2022.
• Corning (GLW.N, Meta Marshall): Corning's display business continues to operate
efficiently and should benefit from further margin support through additional

currency-related price increases. Its optical communications business is also
returning to growth as carrier fiber deployments accelerate. Corning's display
business remains closely correlated with panel maker utilization rates and is the
company's largest earnings contributor.
• Greater China DDIC supply chain implications – Novatek (3034.TW, Daniel Yen):
The Underweight thesis reflects weaker PC, smartphone and automotive demand,
gross margin pressure, and a lack of new growth drivers. That said, Novatek's

OLED DDI roadmap appears more competitive than those of its key Korean peers
in terms of both performance and cost, helping it gain share in Apple's iPhone
supply chain.

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
Where We Could Be Wrong
• Stronger end-demand: If end-demand for major applications turns out to be
better than expected, there could be stronger panel shipments and less panel price
pressure.
• More stringent output control: If major panel makers collectively adjust
utilization to an extreme extent, they could mitigate pricing pressure in the case of
softer demand.
• Non-commodity display business: If the non-commodity business progresses

faster than expected, panel makers could show better financial results.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

2026/08/21 投資速報

營收走勢圖

研究員/王建智 MICHAELWANG82121188@UNI-
PSG.COM

研究部對華航看法轉趨正向，係因：(1) 客運需求維持穩定，暑
假旺季後載客率仍具支撐。(2) 2H26貨運展望強勁，AI、半導體
及資通訊產品需求延續 ， 加上消費性電子及年底旺季拉貨 ， 支撐
貨運需求及運價。(3) 航空燃油價格自高點回落 ，2H26燃油成本
壓力明顯緩解，並有利2027年獲利成長。預估2026/2027年稅後
EPS2.19元（-8.6%YoY）/2.85元（+30.3%YoY） 。歷史本淨比區間
為0.7～2.4倍 ， 考量客貨運需求穩健及燃油成本下降 ， 預期獲利
動能改善 。 故以2027年底每股淨值19.48元為評價基礎 ， 給予1.2
倍本淨比，目標價23.5元，投資評等調升至買進。

重點摘要： 
1. 2Q26高油價壓抑獲利，財報仍優於預期 
華航2Q26營收638.19億元 ，+12.0%QoQ/+24.3%YoY， 毛利率11.1%，
-9.5pts QoQ/-7.6pts YoY，稅後淨利14.13億元，-72.7%QoQ/-
63.4%YoY，稅後EPS 0.23元，優於研究部原先預估的0.08元。

華航2Q26用油量約407.4萬桶 ， 燃油成本約244.2億元 ， 較1Q26增
加114.4億元（+88.1%QoQ） ，為獲利QoQ下滑主因。

表 1：2Q26 財報預估值與實際值差異分析 
 實際值 預估值 差異 
營收 63,819  63,668  +0.2% 
毛利率 11.1% 9.7% +1.4pt 
營益率 4.2% 1.3% +2.9pt 
稅後淨利 1,413  470  +200.8% 
EPS 0.23  0.08    
資料來源：統一投顧整理 
2. 2H26客運需求維持穩定，亞太旅運成長提供支撐 
2H26客運方面 ，IATA預估2026年全球客運需求+2.1%YoY， 高於運
能+1.6%YoY， 市場供需維持穩健 。 華航8月平均載客率目標85%以
上，9月雖進入暑假後淡季，惟受中秋連假支撐，載客率仍預估
維持80%以上，顯示旺季後需求仍具支撐。運能方面，區域線持
續以東北亞為重點 ， 長程線以北美及歐洲為主要發展市場 ， 並依
市場需求及機隊交付情況評估增班或新增航點 ， 預期2H26客運營
收延續成長。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. 2H26貨運需求強勁，AI及半導體貨源支撐收益 
2H26貨運方面，IATA預估2026年全球航空貨運需求+0.7%YoY，由
於AI基礎建設投資持續增加 ， 帶動伺服器 、 半導體及資通訊產品
空運需求 ， 加上下半年消費性電子拉貨及歐美年底購物旺季 ，預
期2H26貨運需求暢旺、運價維持相對高檔。而華航將透過增班、
包機及收益管理，爭取AI、半導體及資通訊等高附加價值貨源，
預期2H26貨運營運維持強勁。

4. 燃油成本回落支撐2H26獲利改善 
航空燃油價格自4月高峰240美元/桶回落至目前的155美元/桶，
航油裂解價差亦逐步收斂， 預期2H26燃油成本壓力將 較2Q26改
善。以用油成本約遞延一個月反映估算 ，3Q26用油成本均價預估
降至138.1美元/桶。假設航空燃油報價於 4Q26末降至120美元/
桶 ， 預估4Q26用油成本均價140.6美元/桶， 仍較2Q26下降23.1%。

圖 1：航空燃油收盤價與用油成本均價(美元/桶)

資料來源：Bloomberg，統一投顧預估

預估華航3Q26燃油成本約178.9億元(-26.7%QoQ)，4Q26燃油成本
約182.2億元(+1.8%QoQ)。雖燃油附加費將隨油價回落調降，但
因附加費無法完全反映燃油成本變化，故油價下行趨勢仍有利燃
油成本下降，並擴大獲利空間。 
預估3Q26營收633.02億元 ，-0.8%QoQ/+24.4%YoY， 稅後淨利27.63
億元，+95.7%QoQ/-6.5%YoY，稅後EPS0.45元；預估 4Q26 營收
652.01 億 元 ，+3.0%QoQ/+20.4%YoY ， 稅 後 淨 利40.96 億 元 ，
+48.2%QoQ/+10.6%YoY，稅後EPS0.67元。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

整體而言，2H26客運需求穩定、貨運展望強勁，加上燃油成本自
2Q26高點回落，預期獲利明顯改善。預估2026年營收2493.18億
元，+19.2%YoY，稅後淨利134.41億元，-8.6%YoY，稅後EPS2.19
元。

5. 2027年運能持續擴充，油價下滑效益支撐獲利成長 
展望2027年，客運需求可望延續成長，華航客機機隊由2026年6
月底69架增至年底74架，2H26持續引進A321neo、A350-900及
787-9，並同步汰換部分A330及737-800。此外，6架787-10規劃
自2027年起引進，新世代機隊持續擴充，有利可供運能及營收
規模提升 。 搭配亞太旅運需求成長 ， 以及AI、 半導體及資通訊等
高附加價值貨運需求延續，客貨運均具成長動能。

成本方面，假設航空燃油報價維持120美元/桶，2027年用油成
本 均 價 可 望 低 於2026 年 ， 挹 注 獲 利 成 長 。 預 估2027 年 營 收
2,604.58億元，+4.5%YoY，稅後淨利175.15億元，+30.3%YoY，
稅後EPS2.85元，預估2027年底每股淨值19.48元。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

附 錄 二 ： 免 責 宣 言 
© 2010 統 一 投 資 顧 問 股 份 有 限 公 司 版 權 所 有 。 本 公 司 提 供 之 報 告 內 容 係 根 據 本 公
司 認 可 之 資 料來 源 ， 並 基 於 特 定 日 期 所 做 之 判 斷 ， 但 不 保 證 其 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 ， 報
告 中 所 有 的 意 見 及 預 估 ， 如 有 變 更 恕 不 另 行 通 知 。

本 研 究 報 告 所 載 之 投 資 資 訊 ， 僅 提 供 客 戶 做 為 一 般 投 資 參 考 ， 並 非 針 對 特 定 對 象 提
供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與
投 資 條 件 ， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見 。 本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資
料 來 源 ， 但 概 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 作 出 任 何
陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非
（且不應 解 釋 為 ）在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 非 依 法 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 人 士 或
公 司 ， 為 於 該 管 轄 區 內 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 遊 說 。 本 投 資 報 告 內 容 屬 統 一 投 顧 之
著 作 權 ， 嚴 禁 抄 襲 與 仿 造 。

高教機交付進入尾聲，惟評價已達合理區間 
 高教機交付進入尾聲，民用業務成長，預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 0.57 元：
展望 3Q26，高教機預計於 2H26 完成交付；民用業務方面，兩大飛機
製造商在手訂單持續暢旺，合計仍需逾 10 年消化，有望帶動漢翔發動
機業務成長，預估 3Q26 營收 98.45 億元(+7.1%QoQ)。獲利方面，受
到原物料成本上漲影響，漢翔已依個別專案逐步反映成本，惟價格調
整仍存在時間差，且高教機相關罰款預計持續認列，預估稅後淨利 5.4
億元(-44.1%QoQ)，3Q26 稅後 EPS 0.57 元。 
 民用與科服需求延續 ， 預估2026 年稅後 EPS 2.47 元：展望 2026 年，
國防部分，高教機專案 結束後，後續成長動能將轉向軍機維保及無人
機業務；民用受惠全球航空市場需求維持強勁， 科服相關專案皆依計
畫執行中。預估 2026 年營收 379.9 億元(+7.2%Y oY)，隨高教機罰款
影響趨緩，預估稅後淨利 23.3 億元(+217.2%Y oY)，2026 年稅後 EPS 
2.47 元。展望 2027 年，民用業務需求仍維持暢旺，且科服專案認列
比重可望提升，預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 2.13 元(尚未考量無人機專案)。 
 目標價 75 元，維持中立評等：評價方面，以 2Q27F BVPS 計算目前
PB 3.5X， 介於漢翔近十年 PB 1.8-3.6X 中緣 ， 考量評價已達合理區間 ，
且無人機預算審議中， 維持中立 ， 目標價75 元(2Q27F BVPS PB 3.6X)。

漢翔為我國航太龍頭廠  漢翔成立於 1996 年 7 月 ， 實收資本額94.19 億元 ， 截至1Q26 底 ， 員工共5,110
人 。 主要從事軍用航空(包含國內軍機製造維修 、機隊商維 、 軍用引擎業務等)、
民用航空(包含民用飛機機體結構 、 次組合設計加工及國際民用飛機引擎零組件
設計加工及代工製造等 )與工業技術服務(包含創能、儲能、資訊服務及售後服
務等)等業務。1H26 應用別為國防 44%，民用 54%，科技服務 2%。

 展望 3Q26，高教機預計於 2H26 完成交付；民用業務方面，兩大飛機製造商

料成本上漲影響，漢翔已依個別專案逐步反映成本，惟價格調整仍存在時間

Exhibit 6：漢翔近年罰款走勢

Sources : Company，MasterLink 
國防業務進入轉換期 ，民用與科
服需求延續，預估 2026 年稅後
EPS 2.47 元 
 展望 2026 年，國防部分，高教機專案預計 2H26 全數交付完畢，相關罰款影
響可望逐步趨緩，有助獲利結構改善，惟隨專案結束，營收貢獻將有所縮減 ，
後續成長動能將轉向軍機維保及無人機業務。隨軍機出勤率提升，維保需求可
望持續增加；無人機及無人機反制系統相關標案亦陸續開標，其中Jul-26 漢翔
透過與 Shield AI 合作之 V-BAT 垂直起降無人機 ， 取得海巡署約12 億元艦載無
人機標案，預期將依專案進度挹注營收。海外布局方面，美國廠已於 Jun-26
完成 ISO 9001 認證，目前仍處於試產階段，後續將視客戶驗證及訂單導入進
度逐步放量。

民用業務方面，受惠全球航空市場需求維持強勁，主要飛機製造商訂單能見度
穩定，帶動零組件需求維持穩健。又 Jul-26 漢翔於英國法茵堡航展與多家客戶
簽訂總額逾 220 億元之新單，有助提升中長期訂單能見度，預期民用業務將持
續成為營運成長動能 。科服業務方面，除 2Q25 取得台電「花蓮鳳林變電站
161kV 靜態同步補償器(STATCOM)新建工程案」外，2Q26 亦取得「台電 MW
級混氫型氣渦輪發電機組統包工程建置案」統包工程，目前相關專案皆依計畫
執行中。另，水湳廠區微電網應用案場預計於 2026 年上線，後續可望作為示
範場域，進一步爭取國內用電大戶，帶動電力島及相關能源系統建置需求。

神基(3005) 
建議：維持買進 
結論：PER 低估，加上明年殖利率仍偏高，下方風險有
限

一、2Q26 毛利率 、 營益率略優於預期 ， 本業達成率100%，
業外淨收入挹注 ， 獲利達成率101%， 單季稅後EPS 2.52
元，累計 1H26 稅後 EPS 4.26 元 
神基 2Q26 營收 115 億元、+24%QoQ、+17%YoY，毛
利率 31.69%(原估 31.0%)、+0.63pptsQoQ、
-1.89pptsYoY，營收顯著季增，強固型 NB 比重增，惟
毛利率受累產品漲價幅度落後關鍵原物料漲，致毛利率
水準未顯著回升，營業利益 1.36 億元、+54%QoQ、
+10%YoY， 達成率101%， 營益率16.82%(原 16.6%)、
+3.31pptsQoQ、-1.19pptsYoY，費用率 14.87%、
-2.67pptsQoQ、-0.7pptsYoY，業外淨收入 1.08 億元(原
估 0.85 億元)，稅前淨利 20.44 億元、+47%QoQ、
+11%YoY，達成率 102%，稅後淨利 15.73 億元、
+45%QoQ、+12%YoY，獲利達成率 101%，單季稅後
EPS 2.52 元(原估 2.5 元)。累計 1H26 營收 208 億元、
+8%YoY，營業利益 31.94 億元、持平 YoY，稅前淨利
34.35 億元 、 持平YoY， 稅後淨利26.6 億元 、+1%YoY，
稅後 EPS 4.26 元。

二、2Q26 年三大產品線同步季增雙位數，其中又以航
太扣件為最，電子產品次之，最後是機構產品 
2Q26 營收比重，電子產品占 54%(其中四成來自國防、
車用占雙位數 、 公共事業占高個位數 ， 餘有警消 、 能源、
零售等)、+36%QoQ、+26%YoY，機構產品占 34%(含
綜合機構產品及汽車機構產品約 1/4)、+11%QoQ、
+1%YoY，航太扣件(豐達科技)占 12%、+14%QoQ、
+40%YoY。三大產品均有雙位數季成長，尤以電子產品
為最，航太扣件次之，機構產品亦有低雙位數成長。 
累計 1H26 年營收比重，電子產品占52%、+14%YoY，
機構產品占 36%、-7%YoY， 航太扣件占12%、+35%YoY，
三大產品線以電子產品及航太扣件年增雙位數為最，餘
機構產品衰退幅度逐步收斂。 
三、2026 年資本支出約 8~12 億元，其中主要以豐達科
擴產為主，餘 3~5 億元，擴產桃園龜山製造基地 
神基 2023 年資本支出達 5-7 億元，2024 年資本支出達
14.6 億元。2025 年資本支出 18 億元，其中神基 9 億多
購買設備及越南廠房，餘 9 億元來自豐達科的馬來西亞
廠及設備等支出。2026 年資本支出預將落在 8~12 億元
左右，其中豐達科資本支出較高，神基 3~5 億元，主要
支出是桃園龜山設立一個製造基地，整修完後擴張強固
型電腦生產製造，Server 機架及電池封裝 BBU 亦有發
展。 
四、展望 2026 年，強固型電腦仍有雙位數成長，PC 相
關綜合機構件年衰退高個位數至低雙位數，汽車機構件
優於預期亦雙位數成長，推估營收年增 11%，稅後 EPS 
9.34 元 
展望 2026 年，整體營運方面，維持2026 年上、下半年
營收比重分別為 48~50%、52~50%的預期 ， 下半年營運
將優於上半年，全年營收成長 5%至 10%的目標可望順
利達成。其中，強固電腦全年營收仍維持成長 20%至
30%的預期，主要動能除既有軍用市場外，公用事業、
運輸及公共安全等新應用也持續拓展。其中，公司已切
入美國大型大眾運輸客戶，涵蓋巴士、火車及校車等應
用，4Q26 初也將發表新一代產品，後續動能看正向； 
與 PC 相關的綜合機構件受到 PC 產業在漲價循環下，
終端承受能力保守看待影響，全年則預估衰退 8%至
12%，目前表現仍落在原先預期區間，但下半年已出現
回溫跡象。不過神基積極拓展高附加價值應用，在碳纖
機構件方面，目前已有兩家 NB 客戶採用於高階商用機
種，預計 4Q26 開始出貨，AI 機櫃部分，神基桃園龜山
大智新廠已在準備機櫃製造所需的重型設備，產能預計
4Q26 初全面建置完成，後續動能可望逐步加溫； 
車用機構件方面，原本預期 2026 年成長 10-16%，不過
目前整體表現優於預期，預期全年成長幅度有望超標，
成長動能主要來自自動化效益展現，同時也積極布局車
用高附加價值機構件，近期切入 tier1 客戶相關車用
Domain Controller 表面處理產品，產品單價較既有產品

五、維持買進理由

及 10.74 元，目前股價116.5 元，PER 12X 及 11X 評價

水準，由於每年配息率都高達有 8 成以上，推估明年配
息殖利率仍有高達 6.44%，故股價下方風險有限，故投
資建議維持買進，目標價維持 160 元。

陳玫芬 合格證券投資分析人員 & CFP 
Lisa.mf.chen@yuanta.com 
葉建伸 
jasperyeh@yuanta.com

2Q26 EPS 1.92 元優於預期 3%；3Q26 估營收季減 4% 
2Q26 營收年增 16%至 80 億元；EPS 為 1.92 元，高於本中心預期 3%，主
因(1) 數位相機銷量持續增加，2026 年估計達 400 萬台，年增 46%；(2) 車
載鏡頭市場 去年 低迷 但今年有所恢復 。展望 3Q26，估計營收季減 4%，
EPS 估計為 2.54 元，下修 7%；下修主因運動相機受記憶體缺貨影響而客
戶拉貨較低迷，短期保守以對。 
聚焦於 AI 液冷散熱、CPO 的 Metalens 與人形機器人應用 
亞光佈局 AI 資料中心液冷散熱由旗下子公司精熙國際(持股 100%)與 Frore 
System(矽谷新創散熱公司)合作，以精密加工技術供應水冷板關鍵金屬件，
規劃將於菲律賓新廠生產，惟估計 2028 年之後營收占比才會顯著。亞光佈
局 CPO 主要 FAU 中的 Metalens 採蝕刻奈米技術適合 CPO 應用的折射率，
應用於準直鏡(Collimator)，估計 2026 年底送樣。人形機器人及 AR 智慧眼
鏡等高階光學應用持續佈局。 
高附加價值產品短期尚難貢獻，評價合理維持持有評等 
亞光在玻璃鏡頭技術能力及 Metalens 長期佈局，有機會成為具成長動能之
高附加價值產品，惟 2026 年及 2027 年仍為送樣階段，估計對營運貢獻顯
著時間在 2028 年之後，短期仍以現有產品為主。2026 年估計營收 289 億元，
年增 9%，EPS 為 6.71 元。較先前下修 9%；2027 年估計營收 309 億元，
年增 7%，EPS 為 8.38 元。較先前下修 6%；下修主因高毛利率的運動相機
估計占比由 10%下修至 8%/7%。本中心以 2H26-1H27 累計 EPS 7. 60 元的
20 倍本益比 (前次採 2026 年 EPS 之 20 倍本益比) 給予目標價 155 元，評
價合理故評等為持有。

元大觀點 
⧫  
2Q26 EPS 1.92 元，優於預期 3%因數位相機及車用表現佳；3Q26 估
EPS 2.54 元，下修 7%因運動相機受記憶體缺貨影響。 
⧫  
佈局 AI 資料中心液冷散熱金屬件，Metalens 用於 CPO 的 FAU，以及
人形機器人的光學鏡頭和視覺感測；惟貢獻時點在 2028 年之後。 
⧫  
亞光在玻璃鏡頭技術領先市場，然而 2026 年及 2027 年下修獲利 9%
及 6%，因運動相機更加保守，目前評價合理給予持有評等。

### Page 2

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：電子零組件 2026 年 8 月 19 日 Asia Optical 第2 頁，共 15頁  
營運分析  
2Q26 EPS 1.92 元優於預期 3%；3Q26 估營收季減 4%且下修獲利 7% 
2Q26 營收年增 16%至 80 億元；EPS 為 1.92 元，高於本中心預期 3%，主因(1) 數位相機(使用
DDR3 記憶體，較不受缺貨影響)銷量持續增加，2026 年估計達 400 萬台(2024/2025 年 136 萬/274
萬台)，年增 46%；數位相機具備光學變焦較手機 的拍照效果更好，體積小且價格低廉受到年輕族
群歡迎。(2) 車載鏡頭市場去年低迷但今年有所恢復。展望 3Q26，估計營收季減 4%，EPS 估計為
2.54 元，下修 7%；下修主因運動相機(使用 DDR4 記憶體)受記憶體缺貨影響而客戶拉貨較低迷，
短期保守以對。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：電子零組件 2026 年 8 月 19 日 Asia Optical 第3 頁，共 15頁  
AI 液冷散熱、CPO 的 Metalens 發展值得長期關注 
佈局 AI 資料中心液冷散熱由旗下子公司精熙國際(持股 100%)與 Frore System(矽谷新創散熱公司)
合開發 3d 液體散熱系統，亞光子公司以真空燒結爐及 800 度的温度來燒結；公司以精密加工技術
供應水冷板關鍵金屬件，規劃將於菲律賓新廠旁建造一座 12 米高且面積超過 1000 平方米的專用
廠房生產，惟估計 2028 年才有較顯著營收貢獻。 
亞光佈局 CPO 包含稜鏡及 Metalens，公司採蝕刻奈米技術，折射率約 3.4 故適合用於 CPO 的
FAU 準直鏡(Collimator)且適用於被動式對位(Passive Alignment)，估計 2026 年底送樣。 
生產基地包含台灣、緬甸、菲律賓、中國大陸；菲律賓二廠進度如預期 
生產基地包含台灣、緬甸、菲律賓、中國大陸 ；菲律賓新建二廠，除原有瞄準器外，再增加車用鏡
頭、射出成形、電鍍、模具等，取代部份中國生產部份；公司規劃增加無塵室、檢測設備及對位設
備。關稅短期影響有限，主因公司產品以零組件為主，僅約 10%營收銷美產品為數位相機 (終端產
品)，關稅由客戶負擔，公司原已規劃緬甸銷美的數位相機等產品由菲律賓廠出貨。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：電子零組件 2026 年 8 月 19 日 Asia Optical 第5 頁，共 15頁  
產業概況  
數位相機市場重返需求，估計 2024-2026 年 CAGR 為 12% 
據日本相機暨影像產品協會(CIPA)數據，數位相機在 2024 年迎來復甦，出貨量止跌回升；全球相
機 2024 年出貨量達到 849 萬 227 台，相較去年成長 10%，繼 2017 年之後再度實現成長，去年出
貨量只有 2015 年的四分之一，但是隨著低價位產品已經被智慧手機取代，當前相機主要瞄準中高
階市場，使出貨額能夠持續推升。年輕族群購買更高性能的相機來取代智慧手機，以利於社群媒體
上拍攝短影片、Vlog 創作，尤其中國更是這方面主要成長動能。估計 2024-2026 年 CAGR 為 12% 
圖 6：數位相機銷量經歷數年衰退，惟估計 2024-2026 年 CAGR 為 12%

資料來源：CIPA、元大投顧整理

圖 7：全球數位相機品牌為寡占市場

車用鏡頭具長期成長性 
據市調機構 Market Research Intellect 研究顯示，2023 年車用鏡頭市場規模為 100 億美元，預計
到 2031 年將達到 197 億美元，2024 年至 2031 年的年複合成長率(CAGR)為 8.2％；顯示車用鏡頭
正快速發展。

機器人應用的工業機器人將持續成長，人形機器人 CAGR 為 81% 
整體工業機器人市場以 CAGR 6.9% 自 2023 年成長到 2030 年，主因五大成長動能，包含 1) 手臂
成本下滑；2) 勞動力短缺與勞工成本提升；3) 中國政策持續出台；4) AI 運算結合機器手臂，提高
產線自動化程度；5) 協作型手臂拓展新應用場域。其中，協作型機器人受惠其具備人機協作且空間
使用小、中國政策持續出台、AI 視覺發展帶動手臂智能化，加速於汽車、電子、物流及半導體自動
化領域布局。 
人形機器人整體 TAM 自 2025 年以 CAGR 81.3% 成長至 2034 年的 280 億美元。主因 1) 人形機器
人成本估將以 CAGR-7.8%自 2025 至 2034 年下滑； 2) 2024 年 Nvidia 推出人形機器人平台
GR00T，包含軟硬體與虛擬世界的機器人模擬器，透過 LLM 與 AI GPU 訓練，使機器人自我學習
並建立人形機器人生態系；3) 人形機器人已積極導入自動化程度較高的汽車產業，從自動化走向智

獲利調整與股票評價  
高附加價值產品短期尚難貢獻，評價合理維持持有評等；目標價 155 元 
亞光玻璃鏡片技術為其特點：1) 數位相機持續受惠於短影片拍攝需求估計持續增長，2026/2027 年
成長 40%/35%；2) 人形機器人與日美歐客戶打樣，長期具機會。3) Metalens 用於光通訊 FAU。
估計對營運貢獻顯著時間在 2028 年之後，短期仍以現有產品為主。本中心對於 2026 年估計營收
289 億元，年增 9%，EPS 為 6.71 元。較先前下修 9%；2027 年估計營收 309 億元，年增 7%，
EPS 為 8.38 元。較先前下修 6%；下修營運因高毛利率的運動相機估計占比由 10%下修至 8%/7%。
本中心以 2H26-1H27 累計 EPS 7. 60 元的 20 倍本益比 (前次採 2026 年 EPS 之 20 倍本益比) 給予
目標價 155 元，評價合理故評等為持有。

公司簡介  
亞洲光學為專業光學鏡片、鏡頭廠商 
亞洲光學成立於 1980 年，主要業務內容為各種光學元件之製造，應用在精密學之鏡片、鏡頭為主；產品包括手機相機鏡頭、
數位相機鏡頭 、瞄準器、顯微鏡、雷射測距儀、 影像感測元件 、投影機鏡頭等產品之生產、加工及銷售 。近年拓展車載鏡
頭、監控鏡頭、街景鏡頭市場，且雷射感測組亦應手於 IOT 智能家電領域。生產基地位於台灣、菲律賓、緬甸、中國大陸 。

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 亞光整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於中風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名於中間的位置，在電
子硬體行業的公司中排名也位於中間名次。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 亞光的整體曝險屬於低風險，該子產業的主要曝險範圍為商業道德、資料隱私安全以及人力資
本。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：亞光在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬中等。公司已採取一些措施來管理
與重大環境、社會和治理（ESG）問題相關的風險，但在關鍵領域缺乏相應的政策和方案。儘管如此，該公司尚未捲入
任何具有重大財務影響的風險事件。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

註：元大給予個股之目標價係依 12 個月投資期間計算。大中華探索系列報告並無正式之 12 個月目標價，其投資建議乃根據分析師報告中之指定期間分析
而得。

1 
3042 晶技(TXC)

報告日期： 2026/08/24 研 究 員：洪瑞陽  (Ryan Hung) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一、 2Q26 營收 36.9 億元， EPS 1.62 元， 7 月營收創高   
2Q26 營收  36.9 億元， QoQ +10.8% 、 YoY +9.9% 。毛利率  
33.11%， 營益率 16.82% ， 稅後淨利率 15.09% 。 單季稅後純益 5.58
億元， QoQ +24.0% 、YoY +50.8% ，EPS 1.62 元。營運動能延續
至第三季： 7 月自結營收  13.66 億元， MoM +14.48% 、 YoY 
+22.91% ，為 58 個月新高 ， 歷史第四高 ； 累計1~7 月營收  84.04 億
元、歷史次高。公司並公布  7 月自結稅前  EPS 0.86 元，單月獲
利同步走揚，反推毛利率接近 36% ，重回歷史最高獲利狀況
(2021-22 年消費性電子爆發年 36-38%) ，本次由雙 A 帶動，後市
可期。公司於法說簡報預估  2026 年  AI 占比自  2025 年  11% 
升至  16% 、車用自  26% 升至  28% ，行動通訊降至  24% 、消費
性 14% ；公司於法說表示  7 月光模組營收占比已高於上半年，
且下半年毛利率朝  36% 邁進，接單動能延續。

二、 AI＋車用驅動產品組合結構性升級，獲利成長  
資料中心： 2027 年 800G 將往  1.6T 放量。隨不同  CSP 架構各
自演進，單機所需時脈 (clock) 數量增加，並朝更高參考頻率 (目前
156/312 未來  625MHz) 、更低抖動發展；石英低抖動、高  Q 的特
性正對應此需求， TCXO/OCXO 則確保節點同步。就價值而言，
公司於法說表示前十大光模組廠皆為其客戶，公司光模組相關產
品 ASP 隨世代顯著躍升 ，800G 約 1 美元 、1.6T 約 2~5 美元、
3.2T 約 5~7 美元，公司於光模組的石英市占約 20% 
車用：隨汽車電子化與智能化加速， ADAS 與智能座艙帶動感測
需求，用量上， L2 升級至  L3 每台車約增加  45 顆，L3 以上一
台車的頻率元件用量可達  70~80 顆(傳統車僅  20~30 顆)，整車
滲透率持續向上。公司於法說表示，車用主力在智能座艙與駕駛，
在中國市場石英市占已居首，並持續拓展歐系、日系客戶，寧波
亦設有車用專廠。晶技的產品組合正由傳統消費性向「雙  A(AI
＋車用 )」成長引擎結構性升級：公司於法說簡報預估  AI 占比自  
2025 年 11% 升至  2026 年 16%、 車用自 26% 升至  28% ，並 明
確指引 AI＋車用 2027 年合計占比將突破五成。

三、產品升級為系統層級，成本優勢築高護城河  
近期市場關注的  MEMS 是否取代石英，公司認為只有些許部分
影響，是競爭者但不是替代者，石英成本優勢太大。其論點在於：

MEMS 屬半導體製程、僅做設計而其餘委外，定位落在 clock/ 振
盪器範疇，並非能取代全部石英的技術；純被動的石英晶體僅需
兩隻接腳 (2-pin) 、單顆約  4~5 美分， MEMS 因結構上必含  
ASIC ，故 MEMS 應對標  TCXO/XO 一類主動產品而非裸晶體。
就成本結構而言， MEMS 屬半導體，一次  12 吋光罩 (tape-out)
投入動輒約上百萬美元、失敗難以回收，石英的成本優勢就顯現。
公司並指出， MEMS 業者多年虧損、近年才因量體出現而轉盈；
其早期能切入光模組，主要憑藉美系光環、強勢行銷與龐大通路，
惟一旦進入亞洲  OEM 供應鏈，客戶對單一供應商的疑慮將帶來
第二供應商的替代空間，晶技如何於早期切入客戶反為需補強的
課題。整體而言，公司視  MEMS 為競爭者而非取代者。

四、預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 7.17/9.03 元 
預估  2026 年營收  150.8 億元、 YoY +13.0% ，毛利率  34.5% 、
營益率  18.5% ，稅後純益  24.6 億元，EPS 7.17 元。2027 年營收  
164.7 億元、 YoY +9.2% ，毛利率升至  36.4% 、營益率  21.5% ，
稅後純益  31.0 億元， EPS 9.03 元、 YoY +25.9% 。隨雙  A 高毛
利占比提升與費用率因規模擴大而下降，營業利益成長幅度顯著
大於營收 (2026 營益  YoY +38% 、2027 +27%) ， 營益率自 2025 年 
15.2% 逐年攀升至  2027 年 21.5%

五、投資建議：買進，目標價  235 元 
考量雙  A 結構性成長、 AI 光通訊與車用題材延續、獲利彈性優
於營收，給予略高於歷史之 26X 2027 EPS ，目標價  235 元 
貳、公 司 概 況 
一、公司簡介  
台灣晶技為一專業之頻率控制元件供應商，自 1983 年成立以來，
始終致力於石英元件系列產品之研發、設計、生產與銷售，專事
生產高精密、高品質之石英諧振器 (Crystal Units) 、石英振盪器
(Crystal Oscillators) 等頻率元件系列產品，相關產品皆可廣泛使用
於行動通訊、電信、資通訊及儲存運算設備、物聯網 (IoT) 、智慧
家庭、人工智慧、高效能運算卡、醫療、各類型連接技術、汽車
電子、電動車等應用市場。依據發展策略及市場需求，持續投入
研發資源，積極發展新技術，以“微型化、高穩定度、模組化”
為產品發展方針，擴大在高端應用及高附加價值產品之市場佔  
有率 。 晶技是全球石英元件之產品應用布局最廣 、 營業規模最大、
市佔率為最高，於石英產業居領導地位。全球生產據點涵蓋台灣
平鎮 (先進半導體製程、高頻高精度產品主力 )、中國寧波 (車用專
廠與前段晶片小型化 )與重慶、印尼泗水 (非紅供應鏈基地 )及日本

MHz Crystal( 石英晶體 /無源晶體 )最基礎的被動元件，僅由石英晶
片封裝而成。自身無法發振，必須外接主晶片 (SoC/MCU) 的振盪
電路才能運作；精度較低且易受溫飄影響。為成本最低、體積最
小的基準元件，廣泛用於一般消費電子、家電、 PC 主機板及車載
基本控制板。  
MHz Crystal Thermistor(TSX/ 熱敏晶體 )本質仍為被動元件 ， 在晶體
封裝內直接整合一顆熱敏電阻。對比前者多了「溫度感測能力」 ，
能即時將溫度傳給主晶片進行軟體補償，用低成本與微型封裝達
到接近  TCXO 的高精度。多用於智慧型手機、 Wi-Fi 6/7 、藍牙及  
5G-A/6G AIoT 邊緣裝置。  
XO/VCXO( 石英振盪器 /壓控振盪器 )主動元件，將石英晶片與發振  
IC 整合於同一封裝內 ， 通電即可直接輸出時脈 。 對比前者多了「主
動發振  IC( 與壓控引腳 )」， 擺脫外部驅動 ， 並支援差分輸出(sub-30

fs 極低相位抖動 )與電壓微調。主要用於  800G~3.2T AI 光模組、
Super NIC 網卡、 PCIe Gen 6/7 伺服器。  
TCXO( 溫度補償石英振盪器 )高精度主動元件，內部  IC 內建硬體
溫度補償電路，能自動修正頻率偏置。對比前者多了「硬體自動
溫補電路」 ，克服極端溫差下的頻率失真，精準度大幅提升至  
0.1~2 ppm( 甚至  ppb 級)。廣泛應用於  5G/6G 終端與  RAN 、車
載 ADAS/V2X 通訊、 GPS 定位與  AI 伺服器時間同步。  
OCXO( 恆溫晶體振盪器 )石英元件的最高階形態，內部設有微型加
熱爐 (Oven) 將晶片維持於固定高溫 (如 85 ˚C)。多了「微型加熱爐
(物理恆溫 )」，從被動補償升級為主動控制，徹底消除外界溫飄，
達到級精度與斷網守時 (Holdover) 能力。主要用於  6G 通感一體
(ISAC) 與 BBU 基地台 、 電信骨幹網 、AI 與 PTP 時脈同步設備。

二、 AI 與車用驅動，產品組合轉佳帶動公司成長  
晶技由過去消費性轉往 AI 與車用電子布局：  
其一為資料中心 。 光模組迭代速度遠快於過去五年 ，OFC 上 3.2T 
方案已亮相， 2027 年 800G 將往  1.6T 放量。隨不同  CSP 架構
各自演進，單機所需時脈 (clock) 數量增加，並朝更高參考頻率 (如 
625MHz) 、更低抖動發展；石英低抖動、高  Q 的特性正對應此需
求， TCXO/OCXO 則確保節點同步。就價值而言，公司於法說表
示前十大光模組廠皆為其客戶，公司光模組相關產品  ASP 隨世
代顯著躍升， 800G 約 1 美元、1.6T 約 2~5 美元、3.2T 約 5~7 
美元，公司於光模組的石英市占約 20%，1.6T/3.2T 多處於打樣送
樣階段。至於  625MHz 產品，公司表示仍在開發與，屬一至二年
後的產品，尚無客戶量產。  
其二為  6G 。相較過往僅承載資訊傳輸， 6G 新增感測 (sensing) 功
能，客戶規格選擇朝更高頻、更嚴格演進，數量穩定成長之餘，
對單一元件的價值要求亦提升。與之相關的低軌衛星，公司視為  
6G 布局的一環，延續  5G 、且  Amazon 、Tesla 等大舉布建；晶
技長期為主要供應商、送樣跟進緊密，惟目前營收比重仍低，公
司傾向將其歸入 AI 佔比。  
其三為車用。隨汽車電子化與智能化加速， ADAS 與智能座艙帶
動感測需求，車輛  E/E 架構由分散式  ECU 往中央集中運算演
進；用量上， L2 升級至  L3 每台車約增加  45 顆，L3 以上一台
車的頻率元件用量可達  70~80 顆(傳統車僅  20~30 顆)，整車滲
透率持續向上。公司於法說表示，車用主力在智能座艙與駕駛，
在中國市場石英市占已居首，並持續拓展歐系、日系客戶，寧波
亦設有車用專廠。  
其四為邊緣  AI 與機器人。此類應用同樣需要晶體與晶振，並要

五成；同時行動通訊主動降至  24% 、消費性降至  14% ，聚焦關鍵
客戶的質量提升。 2026 年資本支出達  10.78 億元，其中約八成
投入  AI 相關產品，桃園廠區並投入  6.9 億元擴充高頻、高精
度、低相位噪聲產品產能，寧波廠擴充前段晶片製程、小型化與
車用產能，印尼廠則對應非紅需求。  
圖四、新產品往高頻、小型化、低抖動、高 Q 值發展

三、產品升級為系統層級，成本優勢築高護城河  
公司於法說表示，頻率元件已不再只是「器件」 ，其提供的頻率也
不再只是一項規格，而是整個  AI 生態系的系統級條件，如同系
統的心臟。公司四年前即提出生態系概念，三年前進一步歸納出
「運算、連接、同步」三大主軸，三者都必須仰賴頻率元件。展
望未來三至五年，一個確定的趨勢是：運算越來越分散 ( 散到  
GPU/CPU/DPU/ 光模組等多晶片多節點 )、傳輸越來越收斂，運算
能力越強、延遲要求越低，對同步的要求也越來越嚴苛這正是頻
率元件需求量價都增長的原因，同步發生的結構性來源，且無單
一器件規格可滿足整個生態系。

石英的技術優勢正對應此趨勢。高速傳輸要求極低抖動與極低相

或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保

位噪聲 ， 石英天生的高 Q 值恰能提供 ； 在同步層面 ，TCXO、OCXO 
則負責確保各運算節點的時序一致。相較之下，晶技的一條龍自
製製程，在交期、品質、供應能力與客戶關係上均較純設計廠具
優勢，亦為客戶採用時的重要考量。公司為全球營收第一的石英
供應商，主要競爭者為台系、日系與中國同業；就日系而言雙方
各有優缺，關鍵在於如何掌握；在中國市場，晶技本身即為當地
供應鏈一環，並居前兩大。

對於近期市場關注的  MEMS 是否取代石英，公司認為只有些許
部分影響，是競爭者但不是替代者，石英成本優勢太大。其論點
在於： MEMS 屬半導體製程、僅做設計而其餘委外，定位落在  
clock/ 振盪器範疇，並非能取代全部石英的技術；純被動的石英晶
體僅需兩隻接腳 (2-pin) 、單顆約  4~5 美分， MEMS 因結構上必
含 ASIC ，故  MEMS 應對標  TCXO/XO 一類主動產品而非裸晶
體。就成本結構而言， MEMS 屬半導體，一次  12 吋光罩投入動
輒約上百萬美元、失敗難以回收，石英的成本優勢就顯現。公司
並指出， MEMS 業者多年虧損、近年才因量體出現而轉盈；其早
期能切入光模組，主要憑藉美系光環、強勢行銷與龐大通路，惟
一旦進入亞洲  OEM 供應鏈，客戶對單一供應商的疑慮將帶來第
二供應商的替代空間，晶技如何於早期切入客戶反為需補強的課
題。整體而言，公司視  MEMS 為競爭者而非取代者。

供應韌性則是第三道護城河，並將地緣政治風險轉為優勢。公司
於台灣、中國、日本、印尼四地布局，策略上讓各廠皆能生產全
面性產品以分散風險；若任一廠區發生供應鏈問題，另一廠區可
於 3~6 個月內承接。其中印尼泗水廠即為因應「非紅 (NCNT) 」
供應鏈需求而設，自設立起即以符合車用  IATF 16949 為目標，
具備供應  Tier 1 的資格；面對美國對中國潛在管制，晶技可同時
提供中國國產化與非紅兩種選項，彈性因應。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估 
一、預估 2026 年稅後  EPS 為 7.17 元， YoY+36.2% 
以 2Q26 實際為基礎逐季推估，驅動因子包含雙 A 放量、光模組
拉貨 (7 月光模組占比升 )、手機 /消費性結構性收斂，並納入季節性
(4Q 營收低於  3Q) 。預估  3Q26 營收  41.2 億元， QoQ +11.4% 、
YoY +18.0% ；4Q26 營收  39.2 億元， QoQ -4.8% 、YoY +18.0% 。
全年營收  150.8 億元、 YoY +13.0% 。毛利率明顯逐季走升 (1Q 
32.33% →2Q 33.11% →3Q 35.80% →4Q 36.20%) ，全年  34.5%(H1 
32.74% 、H2 顯著回升 )。改善由產品組合驅動， AI 與車用高毛利

晶技 (3042 TT)  Trading Buy

進入營運旺季，給予 Trading Buy 投資評等。  
投資建議 ：較高毛利率的 AI 與車用產品營收帶動成長，再加上
較低毛利率的 Mobile 、 NB/PC 頻率元件營收比重下滑，產品組
合顯著轉佳，推動營收、獲利成長，短期進入營運旺季，給予
Trading Buy 投資建議。  
AI 與車用帶動成長， 2Q26 營收 36.99 億元， QoQ+10.77% ，
EPS1.62 元：手機客戶進入傳統淡季，加上 Android 客戶持續下
修出貨量， Mobile 營收季減； PC 客戶搶在半導體零組件大幅漲
價前搶先出貨， PC 相關營收季增；主要受到自駕車的自駕程度
提升，以及智慧座艙增加功能，帶動單台車輛頻率元件採用量
增加；公司加速切入高速光收發模組供應鏈， 800G 、 1.6T 光模
組用震盪器陸續量產，進入 AI Scale-Out 應用將直接受惠 AI 基
礎建設商機， 2Q26 營收 36.99 億元， QoQ+10.77% ，稅後淨利
5.55 億元， QoQ+23.31% ， EPS1.62 元。  
進入傳統旺季，預估 3Q26 營收 42.01 億元， QoQ+13.58% ，
EPS2.21 元： 展望 3Q26 ，進入美系消費性電子客戶傳統新機備
貨旺季，手機、耳機、手錶、平板皆啟動頻率元件備貨，帶動
Mobile 與 Connectivity 營 收 成 長，加 上 各 大CSP 持續建置 AI 算
力叢集， Scale-Out 推動高速光傳輸需求持續成長， 800G 、 1.6T
光收發模組用高頻震盪器持續放量，加上車用產品出貨持續成
長，帶動營收進入年度旺季，預估 3Q26 營收 42.01 億元，
QoQ+13.58% ，稅後淨利 7.58 億元， QoQ+36.50% ，EPS2.21 元。 
AI 與車用帶動營運向上，預估 2026 年營收 153.61 億元，
YoY+15.07% ，EPS7.32 元：展望 2026 年，公司正處於轉型期，
產品營收將逐漸擺脫高度依賴手機與消費性電子的應用，逐漸
轉型為 AI 基礎建設與車用的高規格頻率元件供應商，受產品組
合優化的帶動，高毛利的車用與 AI 產品將主導營收成長動能，
並推升整體毛利率上揚，預估 2026 年營收 153.61 億元，
YoY+15.07% ，稅後淨利 25.09 億元， YoY+39.05% ，EPS7.32 元。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 8 月 20 日

2 群益投顧 
  晶技 ESG ：  
台灣晶技按  ISO 50001 能源管理系統，仍是依循  P-D-C-A 循環管理方法
論，進行系統性盤點、風險評估與檢討，確認業務活動、設備、作業流程、
服務及產品構面，蒐集能源之落點、程度，及時間與活動上趨勢等資訊，藉
由數據分析，以期擬訂有效減少能耗之行動方案，為響應政府推動淨零排放
政策，故持續推動再生能源措施，其中太陽能屋頂光電發電系統單片  230
瓦，共裝置 264 片，總裝置容量為 60.72 瓩，預估年發電量約 63,164 度，目
前仍持續維護、保養與有效運作，未來更再尋求空間，以評估擴增可能性。
AI 與車用帶動成長， 2Q26 營收 36.99 億元， QoQ+10.77% ， EPS1.62 元：  
手機客戶進入傳統淡季，加上 Android 客戶持續下修出貨量， Mobile 營收季
減； PC 客戶搶在半導體零組件大幅漲價前搶先出貨， PC 相關營收季增；主
要受到自駕車的自駕程度提升，以及智慧座艙增加功能，帶動單台車輛頻率
元件採用量增加；公司加速切入高速光收發模組供應鏈， 800G 、 1.6T 光模
組用震盪器陸續量產 ， 進入AI Scale-Out 應用將直接受惠 AI 基礎建設商機 。
AI 與車用帶動成長， 2Q26 營收 36.99 億元， QoQ+10.77% 。  
受惠於 AI 與車用營收比重提升，帶動產品組合轉佳，稅後淨利 5.55 億元，
QoQ+23.31% ， EPS1.62 元。  
進入傳統旺季，預估 3Q26 營收 42.01 億元， QoQ+13.58% ， EPS2.21 元：  
展望 3Q26 ，進入美系消費性電子客戶傳統新機備貨旺季，手機、耳機、手
錶、平板皆啟動頻率元件備貨，帶動 Mobile 與 Connectivity 營收成長，加
上各大 CSP 持續建置 AI 算力叢集， Scale-Out 推動高速光傳輸需求持續成
長， 800G 、 1.6T 光收發模組用高頻震盪器持續放量，加上車用產品出貨持
續成長，帶動營收進入年度旺季。  
進入傳統旺季，預估 3Q26 營收 42.01 億元， QoQ+13.58% 。  
雖然手機相關應用頻率元件毛利率較低，但因 AI 與車用較高毛利率產品的
營收比重，預估 3Q26 毛利率可望優於 2Q26 ，預估稅後淨利 7.58 億元，
QoQ+36.50% ， EPS2.21 元。  
AI 與車用帶動營運向上，預估 2026 年營收 153.61 億元， YoY+15.07% ，
EPS7.32 元：  
展望 2026 年，公司正處於轉型期，產 品營收將逐漸擺脫高度依賴手機與消
費性電子的應用 ， 逐漸轉型為AI 基礎建設與車用的高規格頻率元件供應商 ，
受產品組合優化的帶動，高毛利的車用與 AI 產品將主導營收成長動能，並
推升整體毛利率上揚。  
在車用方面，預計營收比重將由 26% 提升到 28%，躍升為第一大營收來源，
成長動能來自於汽車智慧化，以及智慧座艙與自動駕駛整合的快速發展。隨
著車內感測節點，例如：鏡頭、毫米波雷達、 LiDAR 的搭載數量提升，資料
收集與車內運算速度要求大幅提高，帶動車用頻率元件的用量與規格一併
提升，推動公司營收成長，而車用產品的毛利率高於公司平均，獲利的貢獻
將更勝於營收成長。

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 8 月 20 日

3 群益投顧 
在 AI 應用方面，預估營收比重將由 11% 提升至 16%，是所有產品線中營收
年增率最為強勁的區塊，受到 AI 伺服器機櫃間高速 Scale-Out 連接需求的
帶動，推升 800G 、 1.6T 高速傳輸光模組的需求，高速光模組對傳輸頻寬與
極低延遲的要求，頻率元件必須走向更高頻，例如：從 156MHz 提升至
312MHz 、更低抖動 (Low Jitter) 與低雜訊，此外， AI 運算節點間需要極精準
的同步訊號，所以需要使用震盪器，例如： TCXO 、 OCXO… ，這使得高規、
高價、高毛利率的震盪器成為推升營收、獲利的因子。  
手機、 PC/NB ，受到 AI 吸取絕大多數 DRAM 與 NAND Flash 資源的影響，
記憶體價格漲幅多已上漲數倍，導致廠商不得不漲價反映成本上揚，導致終
端需求收斂，預期備貨旺季過後，此二類營收將加快下滑反需求萎縮狀況。
預期 2026 年手機、 PC/NB 營收佔比預估將下滑。  
AI 與車用帶動營運向上，預估 2026 年營收 153.61 億元， YoY+15.07% 。  
主要受惠於毛利率的 AI 與車用產品帶動營收成長，以及毛利率較低的
Mobile 、PC/NB 營收比重下滑，帶動產品營收比重轉佳，預期毛利率將優於
2025 年，預估稅後淨利 25.09 億元， YoY+39.05% ， EPS7.32 元。  
投資建議：  
較高毛利率的 AI 與車用產品營收帶動成長，再加上較低毛利率的 Mobile 、
NB/PC 頻率元件營收比重下滑，產品組合顯著轉佳，推動營收、獲利成長，
短期進入營運旺季，給予 Trading Buy 投資建議。

  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

精材 (3374 TT)  Trading Buy

測試業務帶動成長，建議 Trading Buy 。  
投資建議 ：台積電為精材的最大股東，持股比率 41% ，也是第
一大客戶，佔精材營收比重 70% 以上。精材對 2026 全年展望維
持正面樂觀，預估 2H26 營運成果將優於 1H26 ，全年營運表現
亦將大幅優於 2025 年。主要成長動能來自晶圓測試業務，受惠
於新廠產能開出與 2H26 傳統旺季稼動率拉升； 8 吋手機光感測
器新專案量產及車用需求回溫亦提供支撐。在 3D 感測封裝
(DOE) 方面，雖受競爭者進入供應鏈影響導致 1H26 營收 YoY-
46%，但減幅目前已逐步收斂並趨於穩定，預期 2H26 起對整體
營收與獲利的負面衝擊將不再出現巨大波動。整體而言，預估
精材 2H26 營收將較 1H26 成長 。 預估精材2026 年稅後 EPS 8.29
元。精材在大股東新單挹注下， 2026 年營收獲利大幅改善，惟
股價已先反應，因此建議 Trading Buy ，目標價 357 元。  
2Q26 稅後 EPS 1.46 元： 精材 2Q26 合併營收 21.85 億元，
QoQ+14.16% 。其中 2Q26 封裝銷貨量 72K 片，較 1Q26 的 69K
片 (約當 8 吋晶圓 )成長 ，QoQ+4.8% ；2Q26 測試服務銷量為 501K
片，較 1Q26 的 424K 片 (約當 12 吋晶圓 )成長， QoQ+18.1% 。
2Q26 毛利率達 27.66% ，較 1Q26 的 29.66% 減少 2.00 個百分點。
毛利率季減主要受到測試服務擴產初期稼動率與 1Q26 相當、
夏季電價成本增加，以及 3D 感測封裝營收季減逾 20% 之影響。
2Q26 精材稅後 EPS 為 1.46 元。  
預估 2026 年資本支出 34.5~36.6 億元，YoY+17.15%~+24.28% ：
精材預估 2026 年全年資本支出 34.5~36.6 億元，
YoY+17.15%~+24.28% 。其中 1H26 資本支出已達 16.18 億元。
在資本支出比重方面，約 80% 用於新廠測試無塵室裝機工程及
廠務設施構建， 12 吋及研發設備約佔 15%，其餘支出約佔 5%。
2027 年資本支出項目主要包括 12 吋晶背銅製程相關設備、部
分研發設備及新測試廠遞延之剩餘工程款，因無大規模產能擴
張需求，預計 2027 年資本支出將較 2026 年大幅減少。

2 群益投顧 
  精材 ESG ：  
精材公司秉持「誠信、創新、客戶導向」的精神追求企業永續發展，除專注
於本業經營外，更堅持落實公司治理與員工、股東、客戶、供應商 /承攬商
及利害關係人建立良好互動管道，善盡企業公民責任。為達成永續發展之目
標，公司將 2016 年訂為永續發展元年，由 ESGCommittee 針對營運活動所
產生之經濟、環境及社會議題，訂定各項永續發展之具體計畫。  
精材以晶圓級封裝技術為核心業務，身為半導體產業之一，將遵循下列行為
準則 (Code of Conduct) 。  
公司承諾：  
1.尊重員工選擇職業之自由，禁止任何形式的強迫勞動。  
2.不使用童工。  
3.提供合理的薪資福利。  
4.尊重員工之基本人權，禁止任何形式之騷擾、歧視或侮辱之行為。  
5.尊重員工根據當地法律自由結社。  
6.減少對社區、環境和自然資源的影響。  
7.遵守法律法規、誠信經營與資訊透明，滿足客戶要求。  
8.保護客戶、供應商和員工的機密資訊和隱私。  
9.持續採取無衝突礦產採購政策。  
10. 強化綠色環保責任、提供綠色產品及建構綠色供應鏈，共同因應氣候變
遷的嚴峻挑戰。  
台積電為精材第一大客戶，佔營收比重 70% 以上 ：  
精材位於封裝中段 Mid-End 製程平台， Mid-End 製程平台包括晶圓凸塊技
術 Wafer Bumping 、晶圓級封裝 Wafer Level Package ，扇出型晶圓級封裝技
術 Fan-out Wafer Level Package ，FOWLP 、晶圓級尺寸封裝 Wafer Level Chip
Scale Package ， WLCSP 、 3DWLP 、 WL Optics 及 3DIC 等技術。  
精材過去的主要業務之一，就是將影像感測器與邏輯 IC，利用晶圓級封裝
製成一顆 IC 。公司專攻晶圓級尺寸封裝服務 (Wafer Level Chip Scale
Packaging ， WLCSP) 與晶圓級後護層封裝服務 (Wafer Level Post Passivation
Interconnection ，PPI) ，2021 年開始增加晶圓測試服務，在 2025 年佔公司營
收比重分別為 45% 、 5%、 48% ，和 2024 年相比，測試營收大幅增加。  
3DCSP 主要應用於影像感測器及環境感測器等光學感測元件。 PPI 主要應用
於功率分離式元件、微機電系統感測器元件、指紋辨識感測器元件和整合式
被動元件等。所封裝之產品廣泛被應用在行動裝置。影像感測器之晶圓級尺
寸封裝及晶圓級後護層 PPI 技術已擴展至汽車、工業、醫療及監控裝置應
用，矽穿孔 (Through Silicon Via ， TSV) 技術亦應用至微機電系統。  
台積電為精材的最大股東，持股比率 41.01% ，精材的前 2 大客戶佔營收比
